<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/*</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * File / folder primitives. &#x3C;FileTreeFolder> and &#x3C;FileTreeFile> are</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * declarative: &#x3C;FileTree> reads them into one flattened, keyboard-navigable</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * collection (WAI-ARIA tree pattern, roving tabindex, typeahead). Density and</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * geometry: 22px rows (under the 24px target floor on purpose: editor density,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * full-width rows, arrow-key navigation), 12px indent per level and one 16px leading slot per</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * row. A folder's slot holds its chevron, a file's slot holds its file-type</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * icon, so at the same level chevrons and file icons share one column. A thin</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * indent guide runs under each open folder's chevron. Rows spring in and out as folders open and the selection</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * is one fill that glides between rows.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileTreeNode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "file"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "folder"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Trailing content (status dot, size, badge). */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  meta</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Input shape for `items` and the declarative parts: `value` is optional and defaults to the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * path from the root (`parent/name`, or `name` at the top level).</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileTreeItem</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">FileTreeNode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"value"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "children"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileTreeItem</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[] }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      out.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">readNodes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((child.props </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }).children))</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> out</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// Typed here so apps without Node types compile; bundlers replace process.env.NODE_ENV at build time.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">declare</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> process</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">env</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">NODE_ENV</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> isDev</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  try</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> process.env.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">NODE_ENV</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> !==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "production"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> false</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Fill in missing values with the node's path and warn (in development) about duplicates. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> resolveNodes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">items</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileTreeItem</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[], </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">parent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">seen</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> Set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>())</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileTreeNode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[] {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> items.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">item</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> item.value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (parent </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> item.name </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> `${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">parent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}/${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">item</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (seen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">has</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(value) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> isDev</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">warn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">`FileTree: two nodes share the value "${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}". <span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    seen.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(value)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">rest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> item</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">rest, value, children: children </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> resolveNodes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(children, value, seen) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> nodes.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">flatMap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">node</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> row</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Row</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { node, depth, parent, pos: i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, size: nodes.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** The file-type icon and tint the tree shows for a file name (by extension). */</span></span>
